# Wiki completeness audit: the verified findings

Generated by the audit lane (docs/prd/masterwrought/phase-20-wiki-audit.md) at execution.
Every row below is a claim in the hand-written guide prose that a fact-checking agent read
against the live tree and an ADVERSARIAL REFUTER, prompted to refute it, could not refute.
The refuter re-derived each one itself; a finding it overturned is in the refuted list at the
end and is NOT re-raised. Severity is the refuter's: `changes-the-page` means a player would
act wrongly on it, `worth-fixing` means inaccurate at low impact, `minor` means a gap in a
descriptive list.

## What was read

| | |
|---|---|
| Rendered surfaces driven | 59 (31 routes, 9 class details, 18 profession sub-pages, the chrome) |
| Audit lanes | 48, each read by two independent lenses |
| Guide keys rendered and audited | 1832 of 1832 |
| Claims classified | 7059 |
| Claims TRUE against the live tree | 6450 |
| Findings raised | 1019 |
| Verified (refuter could not refute) | 763 |
| Refuted or bound | 123 |
| Recorded only (style, unverifiable, bound by the finder) | 124 |
| Distinct keys carrying at least one verified finding | 418 |

Verified findings by severity: changes-the-page 120, worth-fixing 433, minor 199.
Keys by worst severity: changes-the-page 86, worth-fixing 237, minor 91.

9 finding(s) carry no refuter verdict and are listed unverified at the end.

## Verified findings, by key
